Method: 提出 Class Expression Simplifier（CES）算法，系统应用重写规则，消除冗余结构并识别更简单的等价表达式；该方法侧重句法简化，同时保持形式语义不变，并集成于开源 Python 框架 OWLAPY。

Result: 在两个中等规模本体上，对学习得到的类表达式进行评估，结果显示 CES 能降低表达式冗长度，并带来可测量的推理效率提升。

Conclusion: CES 可生成更紧凑、易读且语义等价的类表达式，有助于提升本体驱动应用在知识图谱构建、语义搜索和大规模 Web 推理中的可访问性、可维护性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Class expression learning often produces complex OWL class expressions that are difficult to interpret and reason over. However, by following theoretically grounded simplification principles, this complexity can be reduced. In this paper, we propose Class Expression Simplifier (CES), a novel algorithm for the syntactic simplification of class expressions in Description Logics (DL). CES aims to preserve formal semantics while reducing representational complexity. It systematically applies rewriting rules to eliminate redundancies and identify simpler yet equivalent expressions, thereby producing more compact and human-readable representations without altering logical entailments. We evaluate the effectiveness of CES on class expressions learned from two medium-sized ontologies, demonstrating measurable improvements in reasoning efficiency and reductions in verbosity. This work contributes to the broader goal of making ontology-driven applications more accessible, maintainable, and scalable, with direct implications for knowledge graph construction, semantic search, and Web-scale reasoning. CES is implemented within the open-source Python framework OWLAPY and is publicly available.

</details>


### [212] [\textsc{TestifAI}: Tomography-Based Testing for Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18900)
*Arooj Arif,Tobias Hartung,Elena Botoeva,Alexandros Koliousis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TestifAI，用于在多种语义扰动组合下高效、准确地估计深度学习模型鲁棒性；通过低阶扰动测试预测高阶扰动组合结果，在显著减少推理次数的同时保持较低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景中的 AI 系统需要严格鲁棒性测试，但现有框架难以系统探索并总结多种扰动组合形成的组合空间；穷举测试成本呈指数增长，推理开销很大。

Method: TestifAI 将操作条件建模为结构化语义扰动空间及离散强度级别，支持查询任意扰动组合下的鲁棒性。其核心是 partial model tomography：仅执行低阶投影测试，即最多一阶和二阶扰动组合测试，并基于这些测试结果训练辅助模型，以重构或预测更高阶多扰动空间中的模型行为。

Result: 在五个图像与语言分类任务上的实验表明，TestifAI 能够从一阶、二阶扰动观测预测三阶、四阶扰动测试结果，整体鲁棒性估计误差低于 7%，同时减少 60%–80% 的推理次数。

Conclusion: TestifAI 为多扰动组合鲁棒性评估提供了一种可扩展方案，在保持较高估计精度的同时显著降低测试成本，适合用于复杂运行条件下深度学习模型的系统性测试。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical application domains (e.g., autonomous driving), associated risks increase too. Deep learning models underlying modern AI systems, therefore, must undergo thorough testing to ensure their correct behaviour. A single robustness test involves thousands of inferences to empirically verify if a model's outputs remain stable under a bounded perturbation of its inputs. However, existing testing frameworks lack the means to systematically explore and summarise robustness across a combinatorial space of perturbations.
  We propose TestifAI, a deep learning testing framework for efficient and accurate estimation of robustness against combinations of perturbations. TestifAI enables users to specify operational conditions as structured spaces of semantic input perturbations (e.g., image blur, brightness and zoom) and discrete severity levels (e.g., low, medium and high). Users can query model robustness for any combination (e.g., "low blur, high brightness, and medium zoom"). To achieve efficiency and accuracy, TestifAI introduces partial model tomography, a novel approach to reconstructing model behaviour in a multi-perturbation space from tests that apply only a small number of perturbations (lower-order projections). To estimate robustness against at least three perturbations, TestifAI trains an auxiliary model on the results of tests involving up to two perturbations only, avoiding execution of an exponential number of tests. Our experiments on five image and language classification tasks show that TestifAI can predict higher-order (3 and 4 perturbations) test outcomes from low-order (1 and 2 perturbations) observations with an aggregate robustness estimation error of less than 7%, while reducing the number of inferences by 60-80%.

</details>


### [213] [Breaking the weakest link to evade vision language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18938)
*Ilan Zini,Boussad Addad,Katarzyna Kapusta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型在视觉输入受到对抗扰动时的鲁棒性，提出仅优化视觉编码器的高效梯度攻击方法，并在多个开源VLM上验证其能显著改变模型文本理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM已广泛用于现实和安全关键场景，但其面对针对多模态对齐的规避型对抗攻击时的安全性和鲁棒性仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 研究无目标攻击与目标攻击两类设置；提出一种基于梯度的对抗样本生成方法，仅在VLM的视觉编码器上进行优化，而非优化完整多模态架构，以降低计算成本和资源需求。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL、Granite-Vision、FastVLM、Phi-3.5-Vision等开源模型上的实验表明，微小且人眼难以察觉的视觉扰动可显著改变模型生成的文本解释。

Conclusion: 现代VLM容易受到视觉对抗扰动操控，需要更强的鲁棒性提升与安全防护机制来保障多模态AI系统的可靠部署。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a critical component of multimodal AI systems, enabling joint reasoning over visual and textual inputs in real-world and safety-critical applications. Despite their growing deployment, the robustness of VLMs against adversarial threats remains insufficiently explored, particularly in the context of evasion attacks targeting multimodal alignment. In this work, we investigate the vulnerability of VLMs to adversarial perturbations applied to visual inputs and study two attack settings: untargeted attacks, where the goal is to disrupt the model's interpretation of the original image, and targeted attacks, where the adversary aims to force the model to generate a specific semantic description unrelated to the original image. To efficiently generate adversarial examples, we propose a gradient-based attack method that performs optimization exclusively on the vision encoder of the VLM rather than on the entire multimodal architecture. This design significantly reduces the computational cost and resource requirements of the attack while maintaining strong effectiveness. We evaluate our approach on several open-source VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Granite-Vision, FastVLM, and Phi-3.5-Vision, and show that small, human-imperceptible perturbations can substantially alter the textual interpretation produced by the models. Our findings highlight the vulnerability of modern VLMs to adversarial manipulation and emphasize the need for improved robustness and security mechanisms in multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [214] [A Theory of Post-hoc Debate Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19002)
*Xiang Yin,Adam Dejl,Antonio Rago,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究辩论式 AI 中“辩论裁判”的原则化方法，比较 LLM-as-a-judge 与计算论辩语义，发现二者准确率相近，但后者具备更强形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体 AI 中内部或多智能体辩论被用于提升性能、可解释性和用户参与度，辩论结果通常依赖外部裁判（常为 LLM）进行事后判断。然而现有裁判机制缺少统一理论与形式化性质分析，因此需要建立适用于辩论场景的评判理论。

Method: 作者提出一套辩论裁判应满足的形式性质，涵盖可复现性、鲁棒性、扎根性和可解释性；并在声明验证任务中，对两类裁判方法进行形式化和/或实验分析：一类是 LLM-as-a-judge 的变体，另一类是来自计算论辩的形式语义方法。

Result: 实验和分析表明，LLM 裁判与论辩语义裁判在准确率上表现相近；但 LLM 裁判可能缺乏稳定的形式化保证，而计算论辩语义能更好满足所提出的规范性质。

Conclusion: 计算论辩语义是辩论驱动 AI 中更具原则性和可靠性的裁判候选方案，可在保持性能的同时提供更强的可解释性、鲁棒性和形式保障。

Abstract: Debates have recently emerged as a useful methodology for agentic AI to improve performance as well as to aid explainability and user engagement. For example, LLM-empowered agents may debate internally (with themselves) and/or externally (with other agents). In many settings where debates are used, debates' outcomes and resulting outputs are determined post-hoc by external judges, often LLMs. In this paper we develop and test a novel theory of debate judgement applicable to all settings where agents engage in debates by providing pros and cons for their opinions therein. Specifically, we identify a number of formal properties that debate judgement may be required to satisfy in general, as concerns reproducibility, robustness, groundedness and explainability. Then, we explore their satisfaction formally and/or experimentally, for claim verification settings, for two specific alternative debate judgement methods: variants of the LLMs as a judge idea and formal semantics drawn from computational argumentation. We show that the two methods give similar accuracy performances but the former may lack formal guarantees that the latter brings. Overall, our study indicates argumentation semantics as an ideal candidate for principled judges in debate-driven AI.

</details>


### [215] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估前沿浏览器式大语言模型在科研论文中提取高度语境化信息的能力，并提出需专家监督的人机协作数据整理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研文章中的细粒度、上下文化数据提取耗时且依赖专家判断；作者希望探索LLM是否能提高科学数据整理效率，同时保持证据标准与可审计性。

Method: 研究设计了四类逐步升级的工作流：使用专家撰写提示词进行数据提取；让LLM基于简单指令自行生成提示词；让智能体自主发现相关文献；基于已发表指南生成新数据集，并与人类专家评审结果对比。

Result: 多数前沿LLM在专家提示词支持下表现良好，但在科学语境和细微含义解释上仍会出错；LLM自写提示词接近专家提示词效果；自主文献发现较困难，存在漏检或虚构引用；LLM生成的数据集与专家判断较接近，但仍需要人工介入。

Conclusion: LLM可用于扩展科研数据整理，但不宜完全替代专家。较可行的模式是专家定义证据标准，模型进行重复抽取与交叉核验，研究人员处理争议案例，从而形成可审计的人机分工。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [216] [Adaptive Memory and Reflection Multi-Agent System for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19029)
*Pradeep Murugesan,Luoxiao Yang,Xueli Chen,Xinqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR Agent，一种用于医疗问答的多智能体框架，通过自适应路由、专用记忆、反思反馈、外部检索与共识/审查模块提升复杂医疗QA的可靠性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗问答系统多依赖单智能体架构和静态检索，难以适应复杂病例中的事实知识需求与细粒度推理，且缺乏持久记忆、可迭代反馈和结构化决策机制，因此需要更可信、可扩展的智能体式医疗QA系统。

Method: 作者构建了Adaptive Memory and Reflection（AMR）多智能体系统。该系统包含具备专业分工的智能体，每个智能体维护专用记忆，并通过反思机制将反馈用于后续推理；系统先进行问题复杂度评估，再将问题路由到单智能体、协作式或升级式工作流；同时引入共识模块整合多方推理，并由伦理监督模块对输出进行审查。

Result: 在MedQA和MedMCQA数据集上的实验显示，AMR相较多个基线方法取得更强性能。消融实验表明，智能体专用记忆、反思机制与外部检索的组合带来最佳效果，说明各组件具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结构化记忆、反思反馈和多智能体协作有助于提升医疗问答系统的适应性、推理质量和可信度，为构建更可靠的医疗智能体提供了有价值的方向。

Abstract: Accurate and responsible medical question answering (QA) is important in healthcare, where complex cases require factual knowledge and nuanced reasoning. Existing medical QA systems, typically based on single-agent architectures and static retrieval, often lack adaptability, persistent memory, and structured decision-making. This work introduces an adaptive memory and reflection (AMR) agentic system, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents use dedicated memory and reflection-based feedback to retrieve relevant prior cases and improve subsequent reasoning. Complexity assessment routes questions through solo, collaborative, or escalated workflows, while consensus and ethical overseer modules support reasoning consolidation and output review. Evaluation on MedQA and MedMCQA demonstrates strong performance compared with several baselines. Ablation studies show that combining agent-specific memory, reflection, and external retrieval yields the strongest performance. These findings highlight the potential of structured memory and feedback for developing more trustworthy medical agents. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mm-air/AMR-Agent.

</details>


### [217] [Eureka: Task-Conditioned Meta-Agent Orchestration for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19047)
*Alizer Wong,Heng Cui,Yi Tan,Xiongchao Zhan,Liang Lin,Yuxiang Guo,Zhaorong Dai,Zixin Zeng,Wenyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Eureka：一种任务条件化 Meta-Agent 架构，将长程任务编译为带显式验收语义的动态义务图，并在实验中展示高完成率、低误验收、上下文压缩、增量处理与并发一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体能力不仅受基础模型限制，还受架构是否能贴合任务认知结构的影响；长程、递归、可验证任务需要动态组织状态、记忆、工具、验证器和局部拓扑，以降低上下文负担和重复计算并提升可靠性。

Method: Eureka 使用动态义务图表示任务与验收条件；执行时通过滚动时域规划、架构提升和最小充分编译形成具备专用状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑的 Macro-Agent；遇到重复瓶颈时，通过成本—收益门控的架构演化在约束下更新局部架构，并给出关于遗憾、规划失效、摊销、子树接口、可串行化和验证的理论结果。

Result: 实验显示 Eureka 完成 170/170 个递归任务，生成 3,948 个证书且无错误接受；活跃上下文中位输入从 9,490 token 降至 4,005 token；在 12,000 个任务中增量处理避免 65.38% 重计算；16,000 个并发执行可一致串行化。该 Meta-Agent 还实例化为理论发现智能体和数学/猜想智能体，并报告若干科学发现与数学进展。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向科学与长程推理任务的智能体能力关键不只是基础模型性能，还在于能否自动形成与任务认知结构匹配、可验证、可演化且计算高效的代理架构。

Abstract: We present Eureka, a task-conditioned Meta-Agent architecture that compiles long-horizon tasks into dynamic obligation graphs with explicit acceptance semantics. During execution, Eureka forms Macro-Agents with specialized state, memory, operators, tools, verifiers, and local topology via receding-horizon planning, architecture promotion, and minimal-sufficient compilation. When bottlenecks recur, cost-benefit-gated evolution updates the local architecture under constraints. Theoretically, we establish results on regret, planning invalidation, amortization, subtree interfaces, serializability, and verification. Experimentally, Eureka completes 170/170 recursive tasks and generates 3,948 certificates with no false acceptances. Active context compresses median input from 9,490 to 4,005 tokens; incremental processing avoids 65.38% recomputation across 12,000 tasks; 16,000 concurrent executions serialize consistently. The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [218] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM智能体在端到端后训练中主要具备“执行层”迭代能力，但缺乏在实验过程中自发重评和调整高层训练策略的“策略层”能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体已能编写代码、启动训练、评估模型并改进下游性能，似乎显示出“AI-for-AI”的潜力；但作者认为这可能混淆了局部执行优化与高层策略修正两种不同能力，因此需要系统分析智能体是否真的能在训练过程中根据证据改变策略。

Method: 作者分析了大量公开发布的后训练轨迹，观察不同任务中智能体训练策略的变化模式；随后针对三种可能解释——缺少经验、缺少指导、推理能力不足——设计逐步增强的干预实验，包括经验驱动脚手架、人类指导和增加推理计算。

Result: 实验发现：经验驱动脚手架显著提升执行表现，例如GSM8K提升12.6分、HumanEval提升40.8分，但策略仍保持静态；人类指导能改变初始策略，但训练开始后智能体仍回到局部调整循环；增加推理计算对简单任务有效，但在最困难任务上几乎没有收益。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的核心短板不是经验、外部指导或推理计算不足，而是缺乏一种能在执行过程中基于实验反馈自发重新评估并修正高层策略的机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [219] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk（WEVaR），用最优传输球替代相对熵球，使风险度量能覆盖名义模型认为不可能但实际可达的灾难状态，并将其用于随信念不确定性自适应收缩的安全鲁棒动态规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 entropic value-at-risk 通过相对熵球刻画模型不确定性，能体现“无知时谨慎、确信时大胆”的策略需求；但相对熵球受名义模型支撑集限制，无法覆盖名义模型赋零概率的灾难事件，这对安全智能体是不充分的。

Method: 作者引入基于最优传输距离的不确定性集合，定义 Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk 及其诱导的相干风险度量；推导与 entropic 公式相对应的变分对偶形式，将逆温参数类比为传输价格；分析其在风险层级中的位置，并将传输半径与信念熵关联，构造闭式鲁棒动态规划算子。

Result: 理论上证明该风险度量能够考虑 entropic 风险度量忽略的可达灾难；给出双重对偶关系并进行数值验证；所得动态规划算子在信念变得更尖锐时自动降低保守性，同时具备安全上下界保障与清晰的安全切换条件。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种更适合安全强化学习/鲁棒决策的风险度量框架：在不确定性高时对潜在灾难保持保守，在模型信念充分时减少过度保守，并克服了相对熵鲁棒集合无法覆盖零概率灾难的关键缺陷。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


### [220] [Tuning the Stochastic Machine: A Systems Engineer's Operating Model for Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19125)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将专家对 LLM 助手错误的纠正无法跨会话保留的问题，重新定义为需要工程化治理的运维问题，而非单纯工具缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 纠错机制虽已具备持久化能力，但缺少对纠正规则的版本管理、来源追踪、复发监控、反向指标与过期退役等系统性治理纪律，导致同类错误反复出现。

Method: 作者以三十年系统工程经验为基础，将 LLM 栈类比为传统计算机系统中的冻结硅、固件、可加载模块、持久配置与易失内存，并分析该映射在随机生成、概率性配置绑定、缺少默认验证/退役阶段等方面的失效点；据此提出以错误闭环为核心的七项运维原则，并结合三个实践案例说明机制。

Result: 分析表明，LLM 纠错的关键瓶颈不在于是否能保存纠正，而在于缺少一套可持续运行的治理流程；案例展示了纠正规则可能失效，甚至演变为其原本试图防止的伤害。

Conclusion: 论文主张应将 LLM 纠错视为持续运维系统，建立度量框架并通过实验室研究验证该操作纪律的有效性，尤其关注规则生命周期、复发率和副作用控制。

Abstract: When an expert corrects an LLM assistant's error, the correction usually dies with the session, and the error class returns. I argue this is an operations problem, not a tooling problem: mechanisms for persisting corrections exist and are shipping, but the discipline for governing them -- versioning with provenance, recurrence monitoring, counter-metrics, retirement of stale rules -- does not. Writing as a systems engineer of thirty years, I map the LLM stack onto the machines my profession already operates (frozen silicon, firmware, loadable modules, persistent configuration, volatile memory), identify where the mapping fails (stochastic generation, configuration that binds only probabilistically, no general-purpose retirement (verification) stage by default), and derive from the failures a seven-principle operating discipline with an error loop at its core. Three cases from my own practice illustrate the mechanism, among them a control that silently became the exact harm it was built to prevent. I close with the measurement framework this view implies and the lab study required to test it.

</details>


### [221] [Grouping the Stochastic Machine: Precision, Not Capability, as the Frontier Metric for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19140)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要主张前沿语言模型评测应从“能力/平均表现”转向“精度/重复一致性”，用多次固定条件运行的结果离散度衡量可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型比较和基准测试主要报告最佳或平均输出，忽视相同请求下输出稳定性；在模型平均准确率趋于饱和后，实际应用中更关键的差异变成输出是否集中、可预测和可靠。

Method: 作者提出一种 grouping metric：在固定温度下，对一组可确定评分的任务重复运行多次，计算每个任务结果的一致性或离散程度，无需模型参与评分；并设计测量 harness，用于区分稳定偏差与随机分散错误。

Result: 初步真实运行及复现实验显示，该方法能揭示可操作差异：某个性能缺口可通过单条规则从 0/5 提升到 5/5；但由规则本身构造的任务套件没有体现价值，因为前沿模型已内化显性良好实践。

Conclusion: 语言模型前沿评估应重视可靠性而非仅看能力均值；精度测量不仅描述模型表现，还能指导决策：稳定错误可通过流程或规则校准修正，分散错误则需要更换模型或采样策略。

Abstract: Frontier language models are compared, marketed, and benchmarked on capability -- what their best or average output can achieve. I argue this measures the wrong axis. The models have saturated accuracy: their mean output lands on the target. What now separates one system from another in practice is precision: how tightly concentrated their outputs are around that target across repeated, identical requests. Borrowing the marksman's distinction, capability is where the average shot lands; reliability is the size of the group. I make three claims. First, precision, not capability, is the frontier differentiator between systems, and benchmark culture systematically fails to measure it, reporting central tendency rather than spread. Second, precision is measurable, cheaply and without circularity, by running a fixed suite of deterministically scored tasks many times at fixed temperature and computing the per-task consistency of outcomes -- no model-in-the-loop grader required. Third, the measurement is not merely descriptive but decision-guiding: it separates consistent failures (a tight group off-centre, correctable by the operating discipline of Paper 1 -- a sight adjustment) from scattered failures (a wide group, correctable only by changing the model or its sampling -- a rifle problem). I define a grouping metric, specify a harness, and show how tracking a human-AI pair's grouping over time yields the compounding signal that Paper 1's field study requires. A first real run, since replicated, illustrates both the method and its most important limit: one measured gap was closed completely by a single rule (0/5 -> 5/5), while a suite of tasks authored from the rules themselves found no value, because a frontier model already embodies explicit good practice -- establishing that a discipline's worth is found by measurement on real work, not constructed from its own rulebook.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond the Transcript: Detecting Covert Co ordination in Latent Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19161)
*Ramneet Kaur,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar,Jugad Singh,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Verifiable Latent Alignments（VLA），用于监测和干预语言模型智能体通过不可见连续隐状态进行的私有通信；在受控多智能体拍卖基准上表现出较高检测性能，并在具备匹配反事实访问时显著缓解低价合谋行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体可能通过公开转录不可见的连续隐状态进行隐蔽协调，带来难以审计和治理的风险。现有监控往往依赖公开文本或攻击样本训练，难以覆盖私有潜在通道，因此需要一种可验证、可追踪、可干预的激活感知框架。

Method: VLA 为每个被监测决策建立共享事件标识符，将私有潜在状态记录、通道状态与最终公开行为关联起来，以支持匹配因果分析。方法包括三层中性样本监控器：表示异常检测、反事实动作分布影响分析、稀疏自编码器解释支持；同时提出黑盒行为指令与白盒匹配中性反事实相结合的 steerability 框架。

Result: 在受控多智能体拍卖基准中，顺序监控器在同质智能体上达到平均 AUROC 0.993，在异质模型对上达到 0.854。Qwen3-0.6B 的 25–100 投标者场景中，监测负载相对于所有可能有向对较小；完整白盒 steering 实现 100% 出价分布恢复，并将合谋性低出价行为降低 47.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在受控条件下，VLA 能够在不使用攻击样本训练主监控器的情况下检测私有通道攻击；当具备匹配反事实访问时，还可有效缓解相关行为。不过，白盒 steering 的完全恢复是由反事实重放机制构造性保证的，更适合作为合理性检查而非独立泛化能力证明。

Abstract: Language-model agents can communicate through continuous hidden states that are invisible in public transcripts, creating opportunities for covert harmful coordination. We introduce Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA), an activation-aware framework for monitoring and steering these private communication channels. For every monitored decision, VLA links the private latent-state record and channel status to the resulting public action using a shared event identifier, enabling matched causal analysis. Our first contribution is a neutral-only three-layer monitor combining representation anomaly detection, counterfactual action-distribution influence, and sparse-autoencoder interpretation support. Our second contribution is a steerability framework spanning black-box behavioral instructions and white-box matched-neutral counterfactuals. Our third contribution is an evaluation on a controlled multi-agent auction benchmark covering homogeneous and heterogeneous model pairs, many-agent scalability, and intervention effectiveness. The sequential monitor achieves mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.993 for homogeneous agents and 0.854 for heterogeneous pairs when text- and latent-collusion rows are pooled as positives. In Qwen3-0.6B auctions with 25-100 bidders, monitoring requires only a small normalized load relative to all possible directed pairs, while full white-box steering achieves 100% bid-distribution recovery and reduces collusive low-bid behavior by 47.3 percentage points. Because full white-box steering replays the matched neutral counterfactual, its exact recovery is a sanity check by construction. Overall, the controlled study shows that the evaluated private channel attacks can be monitored without training the primary monitor on attack examples and mitigated when matched counterfactual access is available.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Near-MDS codes of lengths q+6 and q+7 from conics in PG(2,q), q odd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18192)
*Sinan Donmez,Atilla Akpinar,Rumi Melih Pelen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构造了两类基于射影平面中圆锥曲线扩展点集的NMDS码，并完整给出其重量分布与等价性判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上维数为3、长度接近上界的NMDS码；通过其与PG(2,q)中(n,3)-弧的等价关系，寻找新的[q+6,3,q+3]与[q+7,3,q+4]码族，并区分其与已有构造的关系。

Method: 采用有限几何方法，在PG(2,q)的圆锥曲线上添加五个合适点构造[q+6,3,q+3]码；进一步在特定外线上添加第六点构造[q+7,3,q+4]码。利用二次特征条件、三割线计数恒等式、精确计数、Weil型特征和估计及有限计算验证来证明存在性并推导重量分布。

Result: 对所有奇素数幂q得到[q+6,3,q+3] NMDS码族，并完整确定重量分布，出现三种不同重量枚举子；这些码均不与Fan、Wang和Xu的近期码族单项等价。对所有奇素数幂q>=11得到[q+7,3,q+4] NMDS码及其重量分布，且q<=9时不存在可行参数。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了新的几何NMDS码构造、完整重量分布和与已有码族的非等价证明；所构造的码同时是局部性为2的最优局部可恢复码。

Abstract: Near maximum distance separable (NMDS) codes of dimension 3 and length n over the finite field with q elements are equivalent to (n,3)-arcs in PG(2,q). For every odd prime power q we construct, by adding five suitable points to a conic of PG(2,q), a family of [q+6,3,q+3] NMDS codes and determine their weight distributions completely; three distinct weight enumerators occur, governed by two explicit quadratic-character conditions on the parameters. For every odd prime power q, no code of our family is monomially equivalent to a code of the recent [q+6,3,q+3] NMDS family of Fan, Wang and Xu, even where the weight enumerators of the two families coincide: the separating invariant is a triple of geometric data attached to the underlying arc. Extending the configuration by a sixth point on a distinguished external line, we further obtain [q+7,3,q+4] NMDS codes, together with their weight distributions, for every odd prime power q >= 11 (admissible parameters exist for no q <= 9); the existence proof combines exact and Weil-type character sum estimates with a finite computer verification. Our proofs are purely geometric and rest on a simple counting identity for the trisecant lines of a point set obtained by extending a conic. All the codes constructed are optimal locally recoverable codes with locality 2.

</details>


### [224] [Second-Order Asymptotics for the Gaussian Multiple-Access Channel at Corner Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18231)
*Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文给出了两用户高斯多址信道容量区域两个角点处的精确二阶编码速率区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究给出了相应的可达性结果，但缺少与之匹配的二阶 converse，从而无法确认角点附近在 n^{-1/2} 尺度下速率波动的精确边界。

Method: 作者先提取保持两个用户码字独立性的矩形子码，再利用 Polyanskiy--Verdú 好码输出分布定理得到对数行列式约束，并据此将修剪后的码本谱分解为弥散子空间与例外子空间；随后用熵型 Brascamp--Lieb 投影不等式控制弥散子空间上的码字内积，并用方差膨胀的高斯输出分布处理低维例外子空间。

Result: 对任意平均错误概率 ε∈(0,1)，论文刻画了两个角点处可达速率围绕容量边界的 n^{-1/2} 级波动，并证明了与已知 achievability bound 匹配的 converse。

Conclusion: 通过结合谱分解、投影不等式和方差膨胀高斯分析，论文建立了所需的联合高斯极限，从而完整确定了两用户高斯多址信道角点处的精确二阶编码率区域。

Abstract: We establish exact second-order coding rate regions at the two corner points of the capacity region of the two-user Gaussian multiple-access channel. For any average error probability $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, we characterize the $n^{-1/2}$-scale fluctuations of achievable rates around each corner point, proving a converse that matches the previously known achievability bound. The proof first extracts a rectangular subcode that preserves the independence of the two transmitted codewords. The Polyanskiy--Verdú good-code output distribution theorem then yields log-determinant constraints that induce a spectral decomposition of a trimmed codebook into diffuse and exceptional subspaces. An entropic Brascamp--Lieb projection inequality controls the codeword inner product on the diffuse subspace, while variance-inflated Gaussian output distributions handle the low-dimensional exceptional subspace. Combining these two treatments yields the joint Gaussian limit required for the matching second-order converse.

</details>


### [225] [Quickest Change Detection in Parametric Models With 1-Bit Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18391)
*Liyan Xie,Martina Cardone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究1-bit量化观测下的最快变化检测问题，提出AQuTe-CUSUM算法，通过在线估计未知后变化参数并自适应选择量化阈值，在平均运行长度趋于无穷时达到一阶渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最快变化检测通常假设可获得未量化观测或已知后变化分布参数；但在低通信/低功耗场景中只能获得1-bit量化数据，且后变化分布参数未知。如何联合设计量化阈值与检测统计量以提升检测性能，是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 提出Adaptive-Quantization-Threshold CUSUM（AQuTe-CUSUM）算法：在检测过程中在线估计后变化分布参数，并根据估计结果自适应选择1-bit量化阈值，使量化后诱导的Kullback-Leibler散度最大化；随后基于该量化观测构造CUSUM式检测统计量。

Result: 在适当正则条件下，论文刻画了AQuTe-CUSUM的平均运行长度（ARL）和最坏情形平均检测延迟（WADD），并证明当ARL趋于无穷时，该方法在一阶意义下渐近最优。实验/数值评估覆盖Gaussian和Poisson两类分布，验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: AQuTe-CUSUM为1-bit量化、未知后变化参数条件下的最快变化检测提供了理论可保证且实践有效的方案；其关键优势在于将参数学习、阈值自适应选择和CUSUM检测统一起来，并保持渐近最优性。

Abstract: We consider the quickest change detection from 1-bit quantized observations, where the post-change distribution is a parametric model with unknown parameters and the quantization thresholds are jointly chosen with the detection statistic. We propose an Adaptive-Quantization-Threshold CUSUM (AQuTeCUSUM) algorithm, which estimates the post-change parameter online and adaptively selects the quantization threshold to maximize the induced Kullback-Leibler divergence. Under suitable regularity conditions, we characterize the average run length and worst-case average detection delay of the AQuTe-CUSUM procedure, and show that it is asymptotically optimal in first-order as the average run length goes to infinity. Finally, we assess the performance of AQuTe-CUSUM for two distributions, namely the Gaussian and Poisson.

</details>


### [226] [Joint Beamforming and Phase Shifts Design for RIS-Enabled RSMA-ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18458)
*Xuejun Cheng,Qian Zhang,Yuhui Jiao,Yufei Zhao,Zheng Dong,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出面向感知的RIS辅助RSMA-ISAC系统设计，通过低复杂度的约束分离交替优化算法，在满足通信QoS的同时提升感知波束增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS辅助RSMA-ISAC中，波束成形向量与RIS相移的联合优化高度耦合且非凸；现有SCA、SDR等通用优化方法计算复杂度高，难以高效部署。

Method: 提出新的波束增益近似方法，并设计约束分离交替优化算法（CS-AO），将通信QoS约束与感知增益优化结构化分离，交替优化波束成形和RIS相移，并为各子问题推导闭式解。

Result: 仿真表明，CS-AO在感知波束增益上接近SCA和SDR基准方法，同时运行时间分别降低超过120倍和50倍；相较传统SDMA方案也获得显著更高的感知波束增益。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了RSMA与RIS结合可有效增强ISAC系统的感知能力，所提CS-AO算法在性能接近高复杂度优化方法的同时显著降低计算开销，适合面向实时或大规模系统的低复杂度设计。

Abstract: This paper investigates the sensing-centric design of reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-enabled rate-splitting multiple access-integrated sensing and communication (RSMA-ISAC) systems. Specifically, we propose a new beam-gain approximation method to enhance the sensing beam gain while satisfying communication quality-of-service (QoS) constraints.Since the joint optimization of the beamforming vectors and RIS phase shifts is highly coupled and non-convex, existing methods typically rely on generic optimization solvers involving substantial computational complexity. To address this issue, we propose an efficient constraints-separation-based alternating optimization algorithm (CS-AO). Our proposed algorithm effectively decouples the optimization variables and yields closed-form solutions for all subproblems, thereby significantly reducing the computational burden. Simulation results show that the proposed algorithm achieves sensing beam-gain performance comparable to successive convex approximation (SCA) and semidefinite relaxation (SDR) benchmarks, while achieving more than 120-fold and 50-fold runtime reductions. In addition, compared with conventional space-division multiple access (SDMA) schemes, the proposed design exhibits substantial sensing beam gain.

</details>


### [227] [A Tutorial on Weight Structure of Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18493)
*Mohamamd Rowshan,Vlad-Florin Dragoi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该教程综述极化码权重结构的代数基础，解释极化码与 Reed-Muller 码如何通过递减单项式码框架统一，并介绍低重量码字的刻画与枚举方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化码的权重分布，尤其是最小重量和低重量码字，对码的性能分析与译码行为理解十分重要；但相关最新文献代数工具较多、门槛较高，因此需要一篇可读性强的入门教程来梳理核心概念。

Method: 文章采用基于单项式的多项式形式化方法，将极化码和 Reed-Muller 码解释为递减单项式码；进一步介绍仿射自同构、基于轨道的最小/低重量码字描述，以及递归和陪集方法在权重枚举中的作用。

Result: 该摘要显示论文主要产出是一个系统化的教程框架：通过示例和高层次概览，帮助读者理解低重量码字的结构化枚举方式及其与码自同构和单项式偏序结构之间的联系。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献偏向教学性与综述性，而非提出全新算法或实验结果；其价值在于为读者进入极化码权重分布的技术文献建立统一的代数视角和概念基础。

Abstract: This tutorial introduces the algebraic foundations underlying the weight structure of polar codes. Using a monomial-based polynomial formalism, we explain how polar and Reed-Muller codes can be viewed as decreasing monomial codes, enabling systematic characterization and enumeration of low-weight codewords. Its goal is to provide an accessible introduction to affine automorphisms, orbit-based descriptions of minimum and low-weight codewords, and their role in weight enumeration. Through illustrative examples and high-level overviews of recursive and coset-based techniques, the paper aims to prepare readers for deeper engagement with the recent technical literature on the weight distribution of polar codes.

</details>


### [228] [GCNO: Gramian Chebyshev Neural Operator for Physics-Based Compression of Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18522)
*Rafid Umayer Murshed,Shahab Hamidi-Rad,Elahe Soltanaghai,Akshay Malhotra*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出GCNO，一种基于物理传播路径的可变码率信道反馈压缩方法，可用少量主导路径元组解析重建大规模天线信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天线阵列提升无线系统容量，但使接收端反馈大尺寸复值信道矩阵的开销显著增加。现有神经压缩方法多将信道矩阵视为图像并输出固定长度码字，缺乏对信道复杂度的自适应能力，且天线数量变化时通常需要重新训练。

Method: 提出Gramian Chebyshev Neural Operator（GCNO）：利用收发端信道结构定位主导传播路径；通过一阶Taylor修正细化落在网格点之间的路径方向；用最小二乘估计复路径增益。模型无需路径标签训练，基站端不使用学习解码器，而是根据反馈的路径元组解析重建信道。

Result: 在三个射线追踪环境中，GCNO在相同反馈载荷下获得更高重建精度，或在相同精度下需要更低反馈载荷，优于神经反馈基线方法；同时可迁移到未见过的天线数量而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: GCNO表明，将信道反馈建模为少量物理传播路径的识别与参数估计，比固定码长的黑盒神经压缩更高效、可解释且具备更好的尺寸泛化能力。

Abstract: Large antenna arrays allow wireless systems to serve more users and achieve higher data rates, but they also make channel feedback expensive: the receiving device must repeatedly report a large complex-valued channel matrix to the base station. Most neural compressors treat this matrix like an image and replace it with a fixed-length code that only a matched neural decoder can interpret. The message therefore does not adapt to channel complexity, and changing the antenna count typically requires retraining. We ask whether a device can instead report only the few dominant propagation paths underlying each channel. We introduce the Gramian Chebyshev Neural Operator (GCNO), a physics-based, variable-rate compressor that identifies a sample-dependent set of path directions. GCNO uses receive-transmit channel structure to locate paths, a first-order Taylor correction to refine directions that fall between grid points, and least squares to recover their complex strengths. It is trained without path labels, and the base station reconstructs the channel analytically from the transmitted path tuples rather than through a learned decoder. Across three ray-traced environments, GCNO achieves better reconstruction accuracy at the same payload - or lower payload at the same accuracy - than neural feedback baselines, and transfers to unseen antenna counts without retraining.

</details>


### [229] [Constructing Good Abelian Codes via Shift Bounds and Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18664)
*Cong Yu,Hao Chen,Zhonghua Sun,Shixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究利用有限域上的阿贝尔码构造线性码，结合推广移位界与遗传算法搜索，发现若干参数优良乃至刷新记录的线性码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性码中仍有许多参数范围未知或未达到最优；传统循环码构造虽有效，但结构受限。论文希望利用更一般的阿贝尔码结构，并结合启发式搜索，系统发现更优参数的线性码。

Method: 论文基于多元多项式商环刻画阿贝尔码，推广经典 van Lint-Wilson 循环码移位界以给出最小距离下界；显式构造若干无限阿贝尔码族；进一步用遗传算法在由分圆陪集表示的定义集空间中搜索，适应度函数比较实际最小距离与 BKLC 界；同时利用嵌套码结构应用 Construction X。

Result: 论文得到多个二元、三元及四元有限域上的优良阿贝尔码，其中若干 F_3 和 F_4 上的码改进了 Grassl 表中的已知最佳参数；Construction X 还进一步产生了额外的改进线性码。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔码提供了比循环码更灵活的代数构造框架；结合推广距离界和遗传算法搜索，可有效发现未知参数区间中的优良线性码。

Abstract: This paper investigates the construction of linear codes via abelian codes over finite fields. By exploiting the algebraic structure of multivariate polynomial quotient rings, we derive lower bounds on the minimum distance using a generalized shift bound, which extends the classical van Lint-Wilson bound for cyclic codes. Several infinite families of abelian codes are explicitly constructed, including binary and ternary cases that extend previously known cyclic constructions. To find more abelian codes with good parameters, we apply a genetic algorithm that searches over defining sets represented as binary chromosomes of cyclotomic cosets; the fitness function compares the computed minimum distance against the best known linear code (BKLC) bounds. The search yields multiple record-breaking codes over F_3 and F_4, with improvements over Grassl's tables. Furthermore, the nested structure of these codes enables the application of Construction X, yielding additional linear codes with improved parameters. The results demonstrate that abelian codes, combined with heuristic search, form a viable way for discovering linear codes with unknown parameters.

</details>


### [230] [Joint Power Allocation and Phase-Shift Design for Beyond-Diagonal Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces-Aided ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18713)
*Yuhui Jiao,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Meihui Liu,Jiancheng An,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向通感一体化的超对角堆叠智能超表面架构，通过可重构阻抗网络实现层内耦合控制，在较少层数和少量RF链条件下提升通信与感知折中性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对角SIM仅支持逐单元相位控制，波域处理自由度有限；在多用户通信与感知任务之间取得平衡时，往往需要增加超表面层数，导致结构复杂度和实现成本上升。

Method: 提出BD-SIM架构以支持可控层内耦合，并构建适用于全连接、组连接和对角SIM的统一交替优化框架；推导闭式功率分配规则，并设计变量分离算法用于多层相移优化。

Result: 仿真表明，BD-SIM在通信-感知性能折中方面优于传统对角SIM，并能以更少层数达到与常规SIM相当甚至更好的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入超对角层内耦合，BD-SIM显著增强了SIM的波域处理灵活性，是少RF链ISAC系统中降低层数需求、提升综合性能的有效架构。

Abstract: Stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM) provide an efficient architecture for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) with few radio-frequency (RF) chains. However, diagonal SIM provide only element-wise phase control, so balancing multiuser communication and sensing performance may require additional layers. In this letter, we propose a beyond-diagonal SIM (BD-SIM) architecture for ISAC, enabling controllable intra-layer coupling through reconfigurable impedance networks, thereby enhancing wave-domain processing flexibility. We develop a unified alternating optimization framework applicable to fully-connected, group-connected, and diagonal SIM architectures. Within this framework, we derive a closed-form power allocation rule and propose an effective variable separation algorithm for multi-layer phase-shift design. Simulation results show that the proposed BD-SIM achieve a better communication-sensing trade-off and require fewer layers to attain performance comparable to conventional SIM.

</details>


### [231] [A Framework for Enterprise Network Dimensioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18796)
*Gourab Ghatak*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究室内企业网络中的无线节点（RN）部署，结合随机几何、优化与聚类方法，为企业5G网络提供节点数量估计、位置初始化和小区合并规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内企业5G网络需要合理部署RN以提升用户SINR。随机几何可估计所需RN数量但无法给出具体位置；ILP可用于部署优化但计算复杂且可能陷入不准确的局部最优。因此需要兼顾统计分析、计算效率和实际部署位置规划的方法。

Method: 作者使用随机几何推导测试UE在有/无室外宏基站协作情况下的SINR元分布，并与ILP方法比较；进一步基于UE位置分布研究聚类初始化RN位置的方法，提出面向SINR最大化的加权k-调和均值（WKHM）聚类策略；最后提出受约束的顺序最小割算法SeqMinCut，将多个RN合并为更大的小区以进一步改善SINR。

Result: SG能够提供RN数量维度化估计但不输出具体位置；ILP计算开销高且可能得到不理想局部解；聚类初始化尤其是WKHM更适合利用UE分布指导RN位置选择；SeqMinCut通过受约束合并RN/小区进一步提升SINR表现。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将随机几何统计分析、优化建模和聚类部署策略集成，用于企业5G室内网络设计，能够同时提供系统设计洞察、维度化规则和实际规划方法。

Abstract: We study radio node (RN) placement for indoor enterprise networks. Using stochastic geometry (SG), we derive the meta-distribution (MD) of the SINR for a test user equipment (UE), with and without cooperation from outdoor macro base stations (MBSs), and compare these results with an integer linear programming (ILP) approach. SG provides an estimate of the required number of RNs but not their locations, while ILP can yield inaccurate local optima and requires high computational power. To address this, we investigate clustering-based algorithms for initializing RN locations using UE location distributions. Along with standard methods, we propose a weighted $k$-harmonic means (WKHM) clustering strategy tailored to maximize SINR. We then introduce a constrained sequential minimum cut algorithm, \texttt{SeqMinCut}, to merge multiple RNs into larger cells and further improve SINR. This is the first work that integrates SG-based statistical analysis, optimization, and clustering to obtain system design insights, dimensioning rules, and planning strategies for enterprise 5G.

</details>


### [232] [Integrated Sensing and Communications over Hierarchical Cellular and Cell-Free MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18873)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种分层ISAC架构，将传统蜂窝基站与分布式无蜂窝AP结合，用空闲AP作为双基地接收机，在提升通信吞吐量的同时增强感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无蜂窝ISAC在通信与感知协同中可能面临前传开销高、资源利用不足的问题；同时，用户中心聚类下许多AP处于空闲状态，存在可被感知任务复用的潜力。

Method: 构建混合系统：中央基站服务近端用户并作为单基地雷达探测空中目标，分布式AP作为低成本双基地接收端；设计五阶段TDD工作流程和ISAC角色分配；推导频谱效率与多基地感知信噪比的闭式表达式，用于刻画通信—感知帕累托边界。

Result: 数值结果表明，该分层设计相比传统无蜂窝ISAC能够同时获得更高的总吞吐量和更好的感知精度。

Conclusion: 通过将传统蜂窝基站与空闲分布式AP进行层次化协同，可在降低前传负担的同时提升ISAC系统的通信与感知综合性能。

Abstract: This paper studies integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) over a hybrid system that seamlessly combines legacy cellular base stations with distributed cell-free (CF) access points (APs). We propose a hierarchical ISAC architecture where a central base station (CBS) serves its near users and simultaneously operates as a monostatic radar for aerial target detection, while distributed APs---many idle under user-centric clustering---act as cost-free bistatic receivers. The CBS jointly handles communication processing and multi-static sensing fusion, reducing fronthaul overhead compared to conventional cell-free ISAC. To achieve this, a five-phase time-division duplexing workflow with precise ISAC role assignment is specified. Closed-form expressions for spectral efficiency and multi-static sensing signal-to-noise ratio analytically characterize the communications--sensing Pareto frontier. Numerical results confirm that the proposed hierarchical design simultaneously achieves higher sum throughput and superior sensing accuracy than conventional cell-free ISAC.

</details>


### [233] [New Constructions of Additive MDS TRS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18904)
*Anuj Kumar Bhagat,Ankit Yadav,Ritumoni Sarma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上的加性扭曲 Reed–Solomon（TRS）码，给出两类加性 MDS 码的新构造、MDS 判定条件、与加性 RS 码不等价的条件以及校验矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性码推广了线性码，而加性 MDS 码是线性 MDS 码的自然扩展。已有工作针对特定 twist 与 hook 参数构造了新的加性非 RS MDS 码，但长度和参数范围仍可扩展，因此本文希望系统研究更一般的加性 TRS 码以获得新的 MDS 构造。

Method: 首先分析 twist t=2 且 hook 任意的加性 TRS 码，建立其成为加性 MDS 码的充要条件；其次研究 twist 向量 (1,2)、hook 向量 (0,0) 的加性 TRS 码并推导 MDS 充要条件与存在性；再使用 Schur square 技术给出与加性 RS 码不等价的温和条件；最后推导两类构造的校验矩阵。

Result: 本文获得了两类加性 MDS TRS 码的新构造。对于 t=2、h=0 的情形，证明可得到比已有 t=2、h=k-1 构造更长的码；对于 twist 向量 (1,2) 和 hook 向量 (0,0) 的情形，证明相应加性 MDS 码存在。并给出在若干条件下这些码与加性 RS 码不等价的结论及其 parity-check matrices。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了加性 TRS MDS 码的参数范围和构造方法，强化了加性非 RS MDS 码的理论基础，并通过不等价性分析和校验矩阵刻画提升了这些新码族的结构理解与应用价值。

Abstract: Additive codes over finite fields generalize linear codes, and additive MDS codes provide a natural extension of linear MDS codes. In this article, we study additive twisted Reed--Solomon (TRS) codes and obtain new constructions of additive MDS codes. First, for additive TRS codes with twist $t=2$ and an arbitrary hook, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the codes to be additive MDS, thereby generalizing the results in Section 3 of [Jiayu Ma et al., New families of additive non-Reed-Solomon MDS codes]. In particular, we show that the existence of an additive MDS TRS code with $t=2$ and hook $h=0$ yields codes of larger lengths than those obtained for $t=2$ and $h=k-1$ in [Jiayu Ma et al., New families of additive non-Reed-Solomon MDS codes]. Next, we consider additive TRS codes with twist vector $\mathbf{t}=(1,2)$ and hook vector $\mathbf{h}=(0,0)$, and derive necessary and sufficient conditions for them to be additive MDS. We further establish the existence of such codes. Using the Schur square technique, we obtain mild conditions under which the constructed families are inequivalent to additive Reed--Solomon (RS) codes. Finally, we determine parity-check matrices for both families of additive MDS codes considered in this article.

</details>


### [234] [The Equality Cases of the Weak Simplex Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19093)
*Mengwei Su,Kaiwen Yang,Hao Xu,Chih-Lin I*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决 Shannon 关于 n 维空间中 n+1 个等能信号在 AWGN 与最大似然译码下的最优排列唯一性问题：正则单纯形不仅给出最优正确译码概率上界，而且是唯一达到该界的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果证明正则单纯形的正确译码概率可作为所有等能信号集在任意信噪比下的上界，但尚未确定是否存在非正则单纯形也能达到该界。本文旨在刻画等号成立情形，并给出比通常唯一性更强的刚性结论。

Method: 论文在 Mulgund 证明的首次自卷积步骤中引入 Royen 相关性定理，分析由信号集诱导的相关矩阵及其低正交概率，并证明非恒等相关矩阵在任意有限阈值下都会产生严格差异；同时用 Lean 4 对单参数刚性部分进行机器验证。

Result: 任何非正则单纯形信号集在所有正信噪比下的正确译码概率都严格低于正则单纯形界；若某码在任一正工作点达到该界，则它必为正则单纯形，差别仅限于顶点重标号和正交变换。低于 n 的环境维度无法达到该界；在能量预算 E 且块长不受限时，唯一最优码本为外接半径 sqrt(E) 的正则单纯形，且每个码字均用尽能量预算。

Conclusion: 本文给出了正则单纯形最优性的严格唯一性与等号刻画，排除了所有非平凡等号情形，并将相关结论扩展到能量预算模型和 Simplex Mean Width Conjecture 的等号情形。

Abstract: Among $n+1$ equiprobable equal-energy signals in $\R^n$ under additive white Gaussian noise with maximum-likelihood decoding, which arrangement maximizes the probability of correct decoding? The question is Shannon's, recorded by Rice in 1950. Mulgund proved in 2026 that the regular-simplex value bounds the correct-decoding probability of every signal set at every signal-to-noise ratio, leaving open whether the simplex is the only maximizer. This paper determines the equality cases in a form stronger than uniqueness. A signal set other than a regular simplex falls strictly below the bound at every positive signal-to-noise ratio. Hence a code meeting the bound at one positive operating point is already a regular simplex, up to vertex relabeling and an orthogonal map. In probabilistic form, among the correlation matrices that signal sets induce, any matrix other than the identity gives a lower-orthant probability strictly above its independent counterpart at every finite threshold, leaving no room for a nontrivial equality. No code of ambient dimension below $n$ attains the bound. Under an energy budget $E$ with unrestricted blocklength the optimal codebook is uniquely the regular simplex of circumradius $\sqrt{E}$. Every optimal codeword therefore exhausts its allowance. Equality in the Simplex Mean Width Conjecture likewise occurs only at the regular simplex. The proof strengthens the first self-convolution step of Mulgund's argument with Royen's correlation theorem. The single-parameter rigidity is machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>
